815 entries, epitope to equity-research-formatter. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **epitope**: Did a binder land on the chosen epitope, which target residues does it touch, and does it clash? Turns hotspot recall, contact footprint and steric overlap into filterable columns. Load before composing an epitope run, or before believing any statement about where a binder bound or whether it clashes. Traps: a missing hotspot errors rather than scoring recall 0, and `n_clash` counts atom pairs, never comparable with `ringfit_n_clash`. ([jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp](https://github.com/jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/epitope) · ★1)
- **epitope-analysis**: Fast epitope identification and hotspot coverage analysis using distance-based contact detection. Identifies which antigen residues are contacted by the antibody, calculates hotspot coverage, and analyzes per-CDR contributions. CDR positions always come from the design YAML; the skill never guesses IMGT/PDB residue ranges. No PLIP or Docker dependency. ([aurekaresearch/OpenDDE-Harness](https://github.com/aurekaresearch/OpenDDE-Harness/tree/HEAD/opendde_harness/plugin/protein_design/skills/epitope-analysis) · ★136 · 2 copies)
- **epitope-binning**: Analyze directed epitope competition matrices from declared tandem, premix or sandwich assays with matched reference and self-block controls, prespecified thresholds, asymmetric-pair diagnostics, hierarchical bootstrap stability and reciprocal-block graph communities. ([the8thday/AgenticPrism-SKILL](https://github.com/the8thday/AgenticPrism-SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/epitope-binning) · ★1)

([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-figures-and-tables**: Use when building figures and tables for an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) manuscript. It designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-figures-and-tables) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) manuscript against prior work. EPSL's readership spans geochemistry, geophysics, geodynamics, geochronology and planetary science, so the knowledge gap must be framed across sub-fields and against the frontier, not inside one method community. It frames the gap and citations; it does not fabricate references. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-reporting-and-reproducibility**: Use when assembling the supplementary material, data-availability statement, and FAIR data deposit for an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) manuscript. EPSL follows Elsevier's research-data policies and the community expects deposition in discipline repositories — EarthChem, PANGAEA, IRIS/EarthScope, MagIC, NASA PDS, Zenodo. It guides reporting and deposit; it does not generate the data. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-reporting-and-reproducibility) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-review-process**: Use when you need to understand how Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) evaluates a manuscript — subject-editor desk screening against the frontier bar, expert review typically drawn from the sub-fields the paper joins, and editor-led decisions. It sets expectations and hardens the paper before submission; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-review-process) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-revision-and-rebuttal**: Use when responding to an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) revision decision. EPSL decisions are made by a subject editor on the advice of referees who often span two sub-fields, so the response must satisfy each specialist with evidence while keeping the Letter concise. It structures the revision and response letter; it does not fabricate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-revision-and-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-study-design**: Use when designing the sampling, analytical, experimental, or modeling program for an Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) manuscript so the design can carry a process-level claim — sample context, reference materials, replication, blank control, and model resolution planned before data exist. It guides design decisions; it does not execute the campaign. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-study-design) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) via Editorial Manager — letters-format length check, title page and highlights, declarations (competing interests, authorship, AI use), data-availability statement with repository DOIs, and figure/table file compliance. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a result fits Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) and how to frame it. EPSL takes concise, high-significance Letters on physical, chemical and mechanical processes of the Earth and planets, and desk-declines regional case studies without process-level insight, so this skill pressure-tests the frontier claim before you invest. It frames the contribution; it does not run analyses. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Earth-and-Planetary-Science-Letters-Skills/skills/epsl-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **epsl-workflow**: Use when starting or resuming any Earth and Planetary Science Letters (EPSL) manuscript and you need the entry point. - **equity-delivery-assistant**: 股权交割实务助手。律师团队内部使用的股权交割项目辅助 Skill，适用于有限责任公司股权转让、增资扩股、股权回购、隐名股东显名化、股权交割争议处理等场景。提供项目信息提取、交割阶段判断、风险标签识别、材料清单生成、文书范本推荐与起草（内置 35 份成品股权交割文书范本，docx 原件 + 同名 md 参照版双格式，为文书起草的基准依据，见 references/templates/；文本库须覆盖股权交割绝大多数场景，库外文书须按本规则即时生成，不得以"无范本"为由拒绝或只给提纲）、下一步操作建议和律师复核事项提醒。v1.3 起内置《股权交割质检表》（66 项：主表质01—质52＋外部补充补01—补14，基准见 references/股权交割质检表.md），每次输出结果前强制跑一遍质检并形成律师内部底稿（对话内附后或【内部底稿】docx 随附）。当用户提及股权交割、股权转让、增资扩股、股权回购、隐名股东显名化、交割流程、交割风险、交割材料清单、起草协议、生成文书、质检、交割质检等关键词时触发。本技能法律依据以2024年7月1日施行的《中华人民共和国公司法》等现行有效法律法规为准。 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/equity-delivery-assistant) · ★37)
